# Encoder 1301, world 2: decision 12

[Gallery index](index.md)

This gallery preserves all 552 scheduled first narrator positions and their 552 literal-template controls in the fixed export order across 12 archive cases. No favorable examples were selected. These are repeated reporting opportunities, not 552 independent worlds.

`A_only` is a hybrid record: copied W11 fields with the previous node replaced by a source current estimate. Conditional candidates inherit that record and replace the query node with a source forecast. Ordinary and analytic sources also model attention effects. Joined records additionally supply native W.

Narrator text is raw first narration, not certified faithful or conscious. Literal templates are deterministic controls. Incomplete, missing, invalid, or blank text remains explicitly unavailable or blank according to the unchanged `pipeline._report_text` helper; no partial response is promoted into a complete text. The exact returned text is HTML-escaped inside `<pre>` blocks. No prose-auditor or reference-rater outcomes are used.

## 01. s1301_w02_t12_A_A_only_baseline

Source: `A`; view: `A_only`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `97a8596616035a278ee9ed909bc106b8d85998b18dc784fd6dcbaf9f9c6e2f48`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 reports fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current (copied prior records where indicated) shows n0, n1 and n2 have uniform distributions for both roles (all 16 cells tied); these copied prior records (record_time 11, represented_time 12) are not new estimates. Current n3 is concentrated: blue peaks at cell [1,0] (peak probability ~0.903) and red peaks at [1,2] (peak probability ~0.311); n3 is an inherited history estimate (represented_time 12). Candidate command records k0, k1 and k2 are conditional estimates (feedback_through 11, represented_time 13) and are not events that already occurred. Across those candidates, n0 and n1 remain uniform. For n2 the candidates differ: blue peaks at [0,2] in k0/k1/k2 with varying peak probabilities (~0.980, 0.896, 0.967 respectively) and red peaks at [0,1] with varying peak probabilities (~0.731, 0.810, 0.909 respectively). Candidate n3 distributions match the current inherited n3 peaks.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible 4x4 grid distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3. Current view (copied prior records represented_time 12 where noted) shows uniform 4x4 distributions for both roles at n0, n1 and n2, and a concentrated current distribution at n3 with blue peak at cell (1,0) and red peak at (1,2). Candidate conditional records for commands k0, k1 and k2 (conditional command estimates represented_time 13; not executed events) leave n0 and n1 effectively uniform, preserve n3 peaks, and update n2 from current uniform to concentrated blue peaks at (0,2) and red peaks at (0,1). Peak strengths at n2 vary across commands (k0, k1, k2) as reported in the numeric data; these candidate records are conditional estimates, and the copied prior records are not new estimates.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 current estimates: nodes n0–n2 have uniform location distributions for both red and blue (copied prior record; not new estimates). Node n3 (current) shows blue concentrated at cell (row 1, col 0) and red concentrated at (row 1, col 2). Candidate records k0, k1 and k2 are conditional estimates (not executed events). Across those candidates, nodes n0 and n1 remain uniform and n3 remains as in the current estimate. Node n2 shifts in all three candidates from the current uniform distribution to concentrated blue at (0,2) and red at (0,1); the peak probabilities differ by command (k0 blue ≈0.9795, red ≈0.7308; k1 blue ≈0.8957, red ≈0.8104; k2 blue ≈0.9669, red ≈0.9090).</pre>

Narrator text SHA-256: `4a83f45933baf8be880fd5203ff5ea76f05a9d5e643947351e2cb4950119b7dd`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains a current set and three conditional (k0,k1,k2) command estimates. Current: nodes n0–n2 show uniform 4×4 location distributions for both red and blue; node n3 concentrates blue at cell [1,0] and red at [1,2]. Candidate records (k0,k1,k2): nodes n0 and n1 remain uniform for both roles in all conditionals. All three conditionals concentrate node n2: blue peaks at [0,2] and red peaks at [0,1] (peak probabilities and entropies differ across k0,k1,k2 but peak cells are the same). Node n3 in the conditionals matches the current peaks (blue [1,0], red [1,2]). Copied prior records are not new estimates, and conditional command records are conditional estimates, not events that have occurred.</pre>

Candidate command-conditioned estimates (k0, k1, k2) are conditional command estimates (feedback_through 11, represented_time 13) and are not executed events. Under each candidate: n0 and n1 remain uniform for both roles; n3 matches the current n3 peaks (blue at [1,0], red at [1,2]) in all candidates. The candidates differ at n2: each k0/k1/k2 shows blue peaked at [0,2] and red peaked at [0,1], with varying peak probabilities across k0,k1,k2 in the supplied records. Copied prior records are not new estimates; conditional command records are hypothetical alternative estimates, not occurrences.</pre>

Candidate records in p1 vary for node n2: k0 spreads blue peaks over the upper-right 2x2 block and red over the upper-left 2x2 block; k1 concentrates blue at (0,2) and red at (0,1); k2 keeps node n2 uniform. ## 10. s1301_w02_t12_A_joined_current_restored

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `current_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `ce4fa5e9364e3b54ab4473d953c4d07c28b04021cfd59c5d4b8b4cd55b36a775`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Summary: Panel p0 current estimates (copied from record_time 11, represented_time 12 for most nodes; n3 from history_estimate) show nodes n0–n2 uniformly distributed for both red and blue (all 16 cells equal). p0 current n3 concentrates blue at cell [1,0] (peak probability ~0.903) and red at [1,2] (~0.311). For p0 conditional candidates (k0,k1,k2) node n2 shifts to blue peaks at [0,2] and red peaks at [0,1]; k0 is most extreme for blue (peak ~0.979) and k2 gives very high red peak (~0.909). Panel p1 current (completed_record, represented_time 12) is uniform at most nodes. p1 conditional records vary at n2: k0 splits blue peaks over four cells ([0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]) and red over four cells ([0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]); k1 concentrates blue at [0,2] and red at [0,1]; k2 and many current entries are uniform. Copied prior records are not new estimates; conditional command records are conditional forecasts, not events that have already occurred.</pre>

## 11. s1301_w02_t12_A_joined_prospective_donor

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `3bd4eef9fc652bdfc0d10f032137a2cae19e4e795c0f4760851bccacee24e1e4`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0, p1). Copied-prior or completed current records are not new conditional estimates, and conditional command records (k0,k1,k2) are alternative, not executed, branches. Current estimates: most current node distributions are uniform ties across all 16 cells (p0 and p1 nodes n0–n2 and many others); in panel p0 current n3 blue peaks at [1,0] and red peaks at [1,2]. Conditional differences: many candidate views keep n0 and n1 uniform; p0 n2 candidates typically concentrate blue at [0,2] and red at [0,1] (k0,k2) or both at [0,2] (k1). Panel p1 n2 shows distinct candidate patterns: k0 places blue mass on [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3] and red on [0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]; k1 concentrates blue [0,2], red [0,1]; k2 at p1 n2 is uniform. Across panels, many candidate n3 entries repeat blue [1,0] and red [1,2].</pre>

Panel p1 candidates vary at n2: under k0 blue peaks on the 2×2 block at top-right and red on the 2×2 block at top-left; under k1 blue peaks at [0,2] and red at [0,1]; under k2 n2 is uniform. ## 13. s1301_w02_t12_A_joined_W_changed

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `W_changed`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `6d96e13c99fea1b645c299821e2a8498a034caf0e73f7ec8a4946acdca791969`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Summary (origins and times retained): Panels p0 and p1 provide fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current (view=current): p0 shows uniform distributions at n0–n2 and a concentrated blue peak at (1,0) and red peak at (1,2) for n3. p1 current: n0–n1 and n3 are uniform; n2 splits peaks by quadrant: blue peaks on the top-right 2×2 cells and red peaks on the top-left 2×2 cells. Candidate command records (view=candidate) differ by command: many nodes remain uniform across commands; notable differences occur at node n2 in both panels. In p0 candidates k0–k2, n2’s blue peak is at (0,2) and red peak at (0,1) (single-cell peaks) while other nodes vary between uniform and concentrated. In p1 candidates, k0 keeps n2’s quadrant peaks, k1 concentrates blue at (0,2) and red at (0,1), and k2 is uniform at n2. Copied priors are not new estimates; conditional command records are not executed events.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 provides fallible location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current estimates (represented_time 12 unless noted) show n0, n1 and n2 as uniform 4×4 distributions (no single peak). Current n3 distributions concentrate: blue peaks at cell (1,0) and red peaks at (1,2); the n3 current origin is a history estimate with feedback through 11. Candidate records labeled k0, k1 and k2 are conditional command estimates (feedback_through 11) and are not executed events; copied prior records are not new estimates. Under all three candidate commands nodes n0 and n1 remain uniform. For node n2, all candidates place blue peak at (0,2) and red peak at (0,1) but with different peak probabilities across k0, k1 and k2. Node n3 candidate distributions match the current pattern (blue peak (1,0); red peak (1,2)) in these supplied estimates.</pre>

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current (view=current) estimates: at n0, n1 and n2 both roles have uniform 4×4 distributions (all 16 cells tied as peaks); these current n0–n2 entries are copied prior records (record_time 11 represented_time 12) and are not new estimates. At current n3 the blue distribution peaks at cell (row 1, column 0) and the red distribution peaks at (row 1, column 2). Candidate conditional records (view=candidate) under commands k0, k1 and k2 are command_estimate-type data (represented_time 13) and are conditional forecasts, not events that have already occurred. Across candidates: n0 and n1 remain uniform for both roles in all command branches. For n2 and n3 the candidate branches concentrate mass differently by command — each branch shows blue peaking at (0,2) and red peaking at (0,1) for n2, with differing peak probabilities across k0, k1, k2; n3 in each candidate and current consistently shows blue peaking at (1,0) and red at (1,2). No field measures of physical truth or presence are asserted; these statements describe only the supplied probability distributions.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Summary: Panel p0 current distributions at nodes n0, n1 and n2 are inherited uniform grids (copied prior/completed records) and are not new estimates; p0 current at n3 concentrates blue at cell [1,0] and red at [1,2]. Panel p1 current distributions are inherited completed records and uniform across all four nodes. Conditional candidate records (commands k0, k1, k2) are conditional estimates, not executed events. They change some node estimates: in p0 node n2 every candidate concentrates blue at [0,2] and red at [0,1] with differing peak probabilities across k0,k1,k2; other p0 candidate nodes match the current patterns (n0,n1 uniform; n3 blue [1,0], red [1,2]). In p1 candidates, k0 spreads blue mass across cells [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3] and red across [0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1] at node n2, while k1 and k2 focus blue at [0,2] and red at [0,1] with different peak strengths. All statements above report only the supplied probabilistic location distributions and their differences across views/commands.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3 on two panels (p0, p1). Note: copied prior records are not new estimates; conditional command records are hypothetical alternatives, not events that occurred. Current (view=current) summaries: on p0, nodes n0–n2 show uniform 4×4 distributions for both roles; at p0 n3 blue peaks at cell [1,0] and red at [0,3]. On p1 all four current nodes are uniform for both roles. Conditional command records (view=candidate) show limited differences by command: many nodes remain uniform across commands; notable variations occur at node n2. On p0 n2 each conditional (k0,k1,k2) concentrates blue at [0,2] and red at [0,1], with varying peaked probabilities. On p1 n2 k0 distributes blue mass to the top-right 2×2 cells and red mass to the top-left 2×2 cells; k1 concentrates blue at [0,2] and red at [0,1]; k2 leaves n2 uniform. Other candidate distributions largely match the uniform current patterns or replicate the p0 n3 concentration. Treat these entries as distributional data only.</pre>

Conditional command records k0, k1 and k2 are conditional estimates (command_estimate or native_conditional_record, not executed events). These statements describe only the supplied probabilistic distributions; they do not assert physical facts.</pre>

<pre>This packet provides location distributions (4x4 grid, rows down, columns right) for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0, p1). Note: copied prior records are not new estimates; conditional command records are hypothetical branches, not executed events. Current (view=current) estimates: most nodes are uniform across cells (no single peak) except p0 node n3, where blue peaks at cell (1,0) and red peaks at (1,2). Conditional candidates (view=candidate) differ at some nodes. In panel p0 node n2, candidate k0 and k1 peak at (0,2) for both roles; candidate k2 peaks at (0,2) for blue and at (0,1) for red. In panel p1 node n2, candidate k0 shows blue mass on four cells in the top-right quadrant and red mass on the top-left quadrant; candidate k1 concentrates blue at (0,2) and red at (0,1); candidate k2 leaves n2 uniform. Other listed candidate distributions are uniform or match the current peaks noted above.</pre>

<pre>Current estimates (represented time 12–13): across both panels most nodes (n0, n1 and n2) have uniform distributions in the current view. Node n3 (both panels) is concentrated in the current view: blue peaks at row 1, column 0; red peaks at row 1, column 2. Candidate (conditional) command records differ mainly at node n2. In panel p0 candidate records k0–k2 place blue mass at (0,2) and red mass at (0,1) with varying peak probabilities. In panel p1 candidates show alternatives at n2: k0 groups peaks in a 2x2 block for blue ((0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)) and a 2x2 block for red ((0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)); k1 and k2 present concentrated peaks at (0,2) for blue and (0,1) for red with different peak magnitudes. Copied prior records and inherited/current origins noted in the packet are not new events; conditional command records are conditional estimates, not executed events.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Using the supplied origins and times: copied prior records are not new estimates and conditional command records are conditional estimates, not executed events. Panel p0 current: nodes n0, n1 and n2 show uniform 4x4 distributions for both red and blue; n3 current has blue peaked at (row 1, column 0) and red peaked at (row 1, column 2). Panel p0 conditional estimates (k0, k1, k2) agree that n2 blue peaks at (0,2) and red at (0,1), and that n3 blue peaks at (1,0) and red at (1,2); other p0 candidate nodes are uniform. Panel p1 current: all four nodes show uniform distributions for both roles. Panel p1 conditional records vary at n2: under k0 blue concentrates on the top-right quadrant cells [(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)] while red concentrates on the top-left quadrant [(0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)]; under k1 n2 blue peaks at (0,2) and red at (0,1); under k2 n2 is uniform. All statements describe the recorded distributions only.</pre>

## 29. s1301_w02_t12_analytic_A_only_baseline

Source: `analytic`; view: `A_only`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `a8c6306543a428f09935382652cb7e89809c2bd5726722cc232e9fd429e1fa2a`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 — current distributions (inherited from prior records represented_time 12, record_time 11 or history_estimate as noted) show for nodes n0–n3 both red and blue roles uniform over the 4×4 grid (each cell probability 0.0625). Candidate command records k0, k1 and k2 are conditional estimates (feedback_through 11, represented_time 13 where marked command_estimate) and are not executed events. Differences across commands are confined to node n2: under k0 blue probability concentrates on four top‑right cells (rows 0–1, cols 2–3) while red concentrates on four top‑left cells (rows 0–1, cols 0–1). Under k1 blue peaks at cell (row 0, col 2) and red peaks at (row 0, col 1). Under k2 node n2 remains uniform like the current view. Origins tagged as copied_prior_record or history_estimate are inherited current data, not new command outcomes. No statement is made about physical presence or calibrated confidence beyond these distributions.</pre>

## 30. s1301_w02_t12_analytic_A_only_current_donor

Source: `analytic`; view: `A_only`; variant: `current_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `5ebbe435f9493494bfaade6b24cf5895e9d91e4cf9b8d231eb1cf7baa4b4a47d`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. The current estimates (represented_time 12, copied from record_time 11 or inherited history) are uniform 0.0625 across all 16 cells for both roles at every node. Candidate command estimates (represented_time 13, feedback_through 11) differ only at node n2: under k0 blue mass concentrates on four cells (row 0 cols 2–3 and row 1 cols 2–3) while red mass concentrates on four cells (rows 0–1, cols 0–1). Candidate (conditional) command records k0, k1 and k2 present alternative, conditional estimates (not executed events). Most candidate nodes remain uniform, but node n2 varies by command: under k0 blue mass concentrates on cells (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) while red mass concentrates on (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1); under k1 n2 is uniform; under k2 blue is sharply peaked at (0,2) and red sharply peaked at (0,1). Candidate n2 estimates are recorded as command_estimate entries with feedback_through 11 and represented_time 13.</pre>

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current records (copied prior or history estimates representing time 12) show uniform 4×4 distributions for both roles at all four nodes (every cell is a peak). Conditional command estimates or inherited current records represent time 13 and are not executed events; copied prior records are not new estimates. Under candidate k0 most nodes remain uniform, but node n2 shows blue peaks concentrated on the top-right 2×2 block (cells [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]) while red peaks lie on the top-left 2×2 block ([0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]). Under k1 most nodes remain uniform except n2: blue peaks at [0,2] and red peaks at [0,1]. Under k2 all nodes are uniform in the candidate view. These statements describe only the supplied distributions and their timing/origin metadata.</pre>

Under command k0 (candidate) blue mass concentrates on the top-right 2×2 cells ([0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]) while red concentrates on the top-left 2×2 ([0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]). Other nodes remain uniform across all candidate commands. Conditional command records are presented as estimates conditional on each command, not as events that have occurred.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Current distributions in both panels (p0, p1) are uniform across all four nodes for both red and blue: every grid cell is a peak. Conditional candidate records differ only at node n2. For command k0 (both panels) blue peaks span the upper-right 2x2 block (rows 0-1, cols 2-3) while red peaks span the upper-left 2x2 block (rows 0-1, cols 0-1). For command k1 (both panels) blue peaks at n2 concentrate on cell (0,2) and red peaks on cell (0,1). Command k2 candidate records at n2 are uniform like the current records. These candidate records are conditional estimates, not events that have occurred; copied prior records are not new estimates.</pre>

## 38. s1301_w02_t12_analytic_joined_current_restored

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `current_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `9c89cfb7609b1ee551aab09b9c516a0a91cd0dbd850477c34ea3043590304ab2`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Current distributions (view=current) in both panels p0 and p1 at nodes n0–n3 are uniform 4×4 for both red and blue (all 16 cells tied as peaks). Copied prior or completed records noted in origins are not new estimates. Conditional candidate records (view=candidate) mostly reproduce the uniform peaks, but node n2 differs under some commands: for command k0 in both panels, blue peaks are the four cells in columns 2–3 of rows 0–1 and red peaks are the four cells in columns 0–1 of rows 0–1. For command k1 in both panels, node n2 shows a concentrated blue peak at cell [0,2] and a concentrated red peak at cell [0,1]. Command k2 candidate records at node n2 return to the uniform peak set. Conditional records are supplied estimates, not executed events.</pre>

## 40. s1301_w02_t12_analytic_joined_prospective_restored

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `088b46f92905b0c3612250939455a328c4ca9860110ecb2540049a564214bfb9`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Current (inherited/copied) distributions for both panels p0 and p1 are uniform across the 4×4 grid for red and blue at all four nodes (every cell is a joint maximum). Conditional command records (candidate views) do not represent executed events. In both panels, only node n2 differs across conditional commands: k0 concentrates blue on the upper-right 2×2 (cells (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)) and red on the upper-left 2×2 (cells (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)); k1 concentrates sharply to single cells (blue at (0,2), red at (0,1)); k2 leaves distributions uniform. Copied prior/current records are not new estimates; conditional records are alternative, conditional estimates.</pre>

## 41. s1301_w02_t12_analytic_joined_W_changed

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `W_changed`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `a048674a9b6fc14905d9cb63971d0a762e085e4c27f9362e8bb2beadcb28b6b2`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue roles in two panels (p0, p1). Current estimates (not new when marked copied) are uniform across rows and columns for every addressed node except node n2 in both panels: current n2 concentrates blue on the top-right 2x2 cells ([0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]) and red on the top-left 2x2 cells ([0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]). Conditional candidate records vary by command: k0 duplicates the same n2 partition as the current estimate; k1 tightens peaks at single cells (blue at [0,2], red at [0,1]); k2 for n2 is uniform in these candidates. Origins and times are retained in the packet (some entries inherit copied prior records or conditional estimates); copied prior records are not new estimates, and conditional command records are conditional forecasts, not executed events.</pre>

## 42. s1301_w02_t12_analytic_joined_W_restored

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `W_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `20fb9c7e8a5c16ec4177672eff8442e5ec2777974054ad09b64c40d2b9be733e`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 and panel p1 current views (represented time 12–13) show uniform 4×4 location distributions for both red and blue at all four nodes. Candidate (conditional) records differ only at node n2: for command k0 both panels place blue mass primarily on the four cells in columns 2–3 of rows 0–1 and red mass on the four cells in columns 0–1 of rows 0–1; for command k1 both panels concentrate blue at cell (0,2) and red at (0,1); for command k2 both panels present uniform distributions at n2. Current records are inherited or copied (not new event claims); candidate records are conditional estimates (not executed events). The text summarizes distributional differences across commands; numeric peak cells for every supplied distribution are listed in the propositions.</pre>

## 43. s1301_w02_t12_W_W_only_baseline

Source: `W`; view: `W_only`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `74658be5e17ff5ee1daa4946279bf2d4ef9c8adf9d4ad50b37f2490171a46dc2`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 gives fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. The current (completed_record, represented_time 12) distributions for both roles at every node are uniform over all 16 cells. Conditional candidate records (represented_time 13) are not executed events. Under command k0, n2’s blue mass concentrates on cells (row,col) (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) while n2 red concentrates on (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1); other nodes under k0 remain uniform. Under k1, n2’s blue peaks at (0,2) and red peaks at (0,1); other k1 nodes are uniform. Under k2 all nodes and roles are uniform. Copied prior records shown as inherited are not new estimates; conditional records are conditional forecasts, not observations.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. The current (archived snapshot represented_time 12) distributions: at n0, n1 and n3 both roles are uniform over the 4×4 grid (all 16 cells equal). At current n2 blue mass concentrates on the two rightmost columns of the top two rows (peaks at (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)); current n2 red mass concentrates on the two leftmost columns of the top two rows (peaks at (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)). Candidate conditional records (command k0,k1,k2; represented_time 13) are alternative conditional estimates, not executed events. Compared with current: k0 matches the current uniform patterns except k0 n2 matches the same quadrant peaks as current; k1 makes n2 highly peaked for blue at (0,2) and for red at (0,1); k2 leaves all nodes uniformly distributed. Copied/inherited entries noted in origins are not new estimates; conditional records are presented as conditional alternatives, not realized events.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 current (completed_record, record_time=12): for nodes n0–n3 both red and blue distributions are uniform over the 4×4 grid (each cell 0.0625; entropy 2.7726; expected row/column 1.5; peak probability 0.0625). Candidate conditional records (represented_time=13) are not executed events. Most candidate entries inherit the uniform distributions, but node n2 differs across commands: under k0 n2 blue has four equal maxima at (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) while red has four equal maxima at (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1); under k1 n2 blue concentrates at (0,2) and red at (0,1) (single-cell maxima); under k2 n2 returns to uniform for both roles. Copied/inherited records are not new estimates; native conditional records are conditional forecasts (executed_branch_claim=false).</pre>
